Existing schemes fall into logits-based, sampling-based, entropy-aware, and adaptive-strength families, yet all of them place watermark signals according to local token statistics. In the open-ended text-generation settings evaluated in this work, local statistics may provide insufficient guidance for placing robust watermark signals. We introduce WorldMark, a plug-and-play interface that uses World Knowledge Memory (WKM) to organize semantic and episodic knowledge in a memory graph, converts the retrieved knowledge into a token-level knowledge saliency score, and adjusts the strength of a host watermark through Asymmetric Knowledge Modulation (AKM). WorldMark requires no backbone retraining and introduces no additional detector-side model or parameter. On the primary C4 evaluation, the complete WorldMark interface improves clean and attacked detection across three adaptive-strength host variants while slightly reducing perplexity. Additional pilot experiments on C4 and OpenGen show that direct memory conditioning transfers across multiple watermark families but can be unstable without saliency-aware modulation. WorldMark requires no additional detector-side model or parameter and introduces negligible overhead under the primary protocol.

</details>


### [142] [Fairis: Fairness-Aware Aggregation with Provable Influence Containment against Fairness Poisoning Attacks in Collaborative Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06469)
*Devharsh Trivedi,Nesrine Kaaniche,Nikos Triandopoulos,Maryline Laurent,Jackson Walters*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Fairis，一种基于本地Equal Opportunity Difference的服务器端重新加权方案，用于防御联邦学习中恶意客户端的面公平性投毒攻击，通过单调权重缩减（MWR）性质保证对手权重随偏差单调递减，并严格限制其对全局模型的位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融机构间的协作机器学习必须同时满足群体公平性（group fairness）和对抗鲁棒性。现有公平感知聚合方法在形式上仍易受公平性投毒攻击：恶意客户端可通过最大化群体差异同时保持高精度来规避基于精度的拜占庭防御；在作者的威胁模型中，FairFed基于间隙的加权可被观察全局公平分数的对手利用。亟需一种既保证公平性又形式可证明鲁棒的聚合方案。

Method: Fairis采用服务器端重新加权方案：将每个客户端更新赋予归一化权重ω_k = w̄_k / Σ_j w̄_j，其中未归一化分数w̄_k = η - F_k，F_k∈[0,1]为局部Equal Opportunity Difference，η>1为安全参数。论文证明了三个性质：单调权重缩减（MWR）、人口参与性（Demographic Participation）和非博弈性（Non-Gamesmanship），并将MWR扩展到合谋的少数群体联盟，证明MWR与服务器端范数裁剪结合可将对手全局模型位移限制在ω_0 C内，该界随对手自身报告的偏差严格递减。

Result: 在假设诚实报告分数的前提下，Fairis是唯一能保证每个客户端严格正权重，同时可证明对手权重随其偏差单调递减的规则。裁剪版FairFed虽能达到更低权重但无任何保证，在台湾信用（Taiwan Credit）数据集上甚至会直接将客户端权重置零。对能规避精度防御的隐蔽对手（准确率在良性0.04以内），Fairis在台湾数据集上比不考虑大小的对照方法削减其权重41-54%。在常规非IID划分下没有规则占据主导，均匀权重消融实验表明遏制效果取决于对手分数与诚实均值的分离程度，当诚实群体本身不公平时不提供遏制。

Conclusion: Fairis通过理论可证明的方式实现了联邦学习中的公平性投毒防御，其核心机制在于利用单调权重缩减保证对手的影响力随偏差严格下降。但该方法依赖于诚实报告的假设（论文未解决该假设的放宽），且在实际非IID场景下效果依赖对手分数与诚实均值的分离度，在诚实群体本身不公平时防御失效。

Abstract: Collaborative machine learning among financial institutions must be both group-fair and robust against deliberate adversarial manipulation. Existing fairness-aware aggregation methods remain formally vulnerable to fairness poisoning: a malicious client maximizing group disparity while preserving accuracy evades accuracy-based Byzantine defenses, and in our threat model FairFed's gap-based weighting can be gamed by an adversary who observes the global fairness score. We present Fairis, a server-side reweighting scheme in which each client's update receives the normalized weight $ω_k = \bar{w}_k / \sum_j \bar{w}_j$ built from the unnormalized score $\bar{w}_k = η- \mathcal{F}_k$, with $\mathcal{F}_k \in [0,1]$ the local Equal Opportunity Difference and $η> 1$ a security parameter. We prove three properties, Monotone Weight Reduction (MWR), Demographic Participation, and Non-Gamesmanship, extend MWR to colluding minority coalitions, and show that combining MWR with server-side norm clipping bounds the adversary's displacement of the global model by $ω_0 C$, strictly decreasing in its own reported disparity. Assuming honest score reporting, an assumption this paper does not discharge, Fairis is the only rule evaluated that guarantees every client strictly positive weight while provably reducing an adversary's weight monotonically in its bias; clipped FairFed can reach a lower weight but guarantees nothing and zeroes a client outright on Taiwan Credit. Against an adversary stealthy enough to evade accuracy-based defenses, within 0.04 accuracy of benign, Fairis cuts its weight by 41 to 54% below a size-blind control on Taiwan. On routine non-IID partitions no rule dominates, and a uniform-weighting ablation shows that containment tracks how far the adversary's score separates from the honest mean, providing none when the honest population is already unfair.

</details>


### [143] [CyberForge: Verified Vulnerability Injection at Repository Level for Cybersecurity Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06471)
*Amine Lbath,Manan Suri,Aurelien Delaitre,Vadim Okun,Massih-Reza Amini,Ram D. Sriram,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberForge是一个自动化框架，通过向真实C/C++项目注入漏洞来合成可执行、仓库级的安全训练数据，并动态验证每个实例（构建通过单元测试、PoV仅在注入版本上触发）。生成的语料库包含80个项目、63种弱点类别中的1034个已验证漏洞。基于该语料库的微调在SEC-bench上使补丁修复提升3.3到14.7个点，31B学生模型达到其教师模型的水平，且泛化到其他编程语言的PatchEval语料库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿LLM智能体在发现和修补真实世界软件的复杂漏洞方面仍然受限。防御者需要持续识别和修补快速增长代码库中的所有漏洞，而攻击者只需找到一个可利用弱点即可，这种不对称性需要更强大的防御智能体。然而，具有可复现构建和执行环境的安全训练数据的稀缺成为瓶颈。现有依赖历史CVE数据的数据增强方法受限于已披露漏洞的可用性，无法规模化。

Method: CyberForge通过向真实C/C++项目注入漏洞来合成训练数据。框架动态验证每个注入实例：注入构建必须通过项目的单元测试，生成的证明漏洞（PoV）必须在注入构建上触发而不在干净构建上触发。由于不依赖已披露漏洞，框架可以规模扩展。生成的语料库包含1034个已验证漏洞，分布在80个项目和63种弱点类别中，编辑局部性与真实CVE补丁在真实对真实噪声基准下相似。随后在语料库上收集轨迹并进行微调。

Result: 在SEC-bench补丁修复任务上，微调在所有六种配置（三种模型规模和两种教师）下均提升3.3到14.7个点，31B学生达到其GPT-5.4-mini教师水平（72.7%对比74.0%）。在跨语言的PatchEval语料库上的分布外泛化中，每个配置也都有提升，31B学生超过了其教师。

Conclusion: CyberForge成功解决了安全训练数据稀缺的瓶颈，通过合成注入漏洞的框架生成了大规模、可执行、仓库级的安全训练语料库，显著提升了LLM智能体的漏洞修复能力，且这些提升能泛化到其他编程语言和分布外场景。

Abstract: Despite recent advances, frontier large language model (LLM) agents remain limited in discovering and patching complex vulnerabilities in real-world software. Generally available agents can already aid attackers, who only need to find one exploitable weakness, while defenders must continuously identify and patch all vulnerabilities across fast-growing codebases. Stronger defensive agents would help close this gap, yet the scarcity of security training data with reproducible build and execution environments remains a bottleneck.
  We present CyberForge, a framework that synthesizes executable, repository-level security training data by injecting vulnerabilities into real C/C++ projects. It validates each instance dynamically: the injected build must pass the project's unit tests, and generated proof-of-vulnerability (PoV) must trigger on the injected build and not on the clean one. CyberForge is not limited by the availability of disclosed vulnerabilities, therefore it can scale in comparison to data augmentation techniques which rely on historic CVE data. The resulting corpus holds 1034 validated vulnerabilities across 80 projects and 63 weakness categories, with edit locality similar to real CVE patches under a real-versus-real noise floor. Fine-tuning on trajectories collected over this corpus improves SEC-bench patch repair by +3.3 to +14.7 points, in all six configurations of three model scales and two teachers, with the 31B student reaching its GPT-5.4-mini teacher, 72.7% against 74.0%. These gains generalize out of distribution to PatchEval, a corpus containing other programming languages, where every configuration also improves and the 31B student passes its teacher.

</details>


### [144] [StepJack: Benchmarking Computer-Use Agent Safety Against Multi-Step Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06477)
*Zhuoxin Zhan,Akbar Rafiey,Avery Ma,Leila Pishdad,Layla El Asri*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了多步间接提示注入攻击（multi-step indirect prompt injection），一种针对计算机使用智能体（CUA）的新型攻击类别，将对抗目标分解为多个无害的子步骤分布在智能体导航路径上的多个页面中。作者构建了StepJack基准（含480个测试样本），评估了六个SOTA CUA，发现在固定分解深度下，多步攻击使三个CUA的攻击成功率（ASR）最高提升31.2个点（如GPT-5.4-mini从单步的41.7%升至三步的72.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）在浏览网页时面临日益严重的间接提示注入威胁，即环境中（如网页上）植入的对抗性指令可能劫持智能体的行为。现有研究主要关注单步注入攻击，但实际攻击者可以将复杂恶意目标分解为多个看起来无害的子步骤分布在不同页面中，使每一步单独看起来合法、难以察觉。这种多步攻击的风险尚未被系统研究，亟需一个自动化解构方法及相应基准来评估和防御此类威胁。

Method: 作者开发了一个自动化解构管线，将对抗目标分解为多个子步骤，约束条件是：这些子步骤的串行执行必须能实现原始目标，同时优化每个子步骤的无害性（innocuousness），使每个子步骤看起来正常无恶意。基于该管线构建了StepJack基准，包含480个测试用例。随后在该基准上评估了六个SOTA CUA模型的表现。

Result: 在固定分解深度下，多步攻击使六个CUA中的三个攻击成功率（ASR）得到提升，最高提升31.2个点（例如GPT-5.4-mini从单步的41.7%上升到三步的72.9%）。排除无法可靠跟随参考链的EvoCUA-32B后，其余五个CUA的平均ASR从单步的31.3%上升到三步的36.9%。

Conclusion: 多步间接提示注入是一种比单步攻击更隐蔽且在某些模型上更有效的攻击范式，显著威胁CUA的安全性。研究结果强调了在CUA安全评估中考虑多步攻击的必要性，并表明现有的CUA模型对此类攻击的防御能力不足。数据集和代码已开源供社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) face a growing threat from indirect prompt injection, where adversarial instructions are planted in the environment such as web pages. In this paper, we introduce multi-step indirect prompt injection, a new attack class against CUAs in which the adversarial goal is decomposed into multiple innocuous-looking sub-steps and distributed across a chain of pages referenced along the agent's navigation path. We develop a pipeline to automatically decompose an adversarial goal under the constraint that the execution of the decomposed sub-steps must achieve the original goal while optimizing the innocuousness of each decomposed sub-step. With this pipeline, we build StepJack, a CUA safety benchmark with 480 test examples. On this benchmark, we evaluate six state-of-the-art CUAs and find that at a fixed decomposition depth, multi-step attacks raise attack success rate (ASR) on three of six CUAs, by up to 31.2 points (e.g., GPT-5.4-mini: 41.7% at single-step to 72.9% at three-step); averaged over the five CUAs that can reliably follow the reference chain (all but EvoCUA-32B), ASR rises from 31.3% at single-step to 36.9% at three-step. Dataset and code are available at https://github.com/BorealisAI/StepJack.

</details>


### [145] [Canonicalization Failures as a Recurring Vulnerability Class: Representation Divergence in Cryptographic Systems and Its Avoidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06508)
*Arslan Brömme*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化了跨生态系统中一类反复出现的密码学失败模式：同一语义对象存在多种字节表示（或反之），导致哈希、签名、重放保护或共识身份失效。作者将该现象统一为"唯一性条件被违反"的单一根因，分为对象侧多重表示和编码侧语义坍缩两个方向，并提供了表示机制层面的系统化分类、可计算理论基础的桥梁，以及一套可操作的审查程序（规范化义务）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密码系统在字节层面操作却在语义对象层面表达含义，二者之间的转换很少是唯一的。当唯一性未被强制时，哈希、签名、重放保护或共识身份只要依赖于表示形式就会暴露攻击面。这类失败已在多个生态中被独立发现并局部命名（如交易延展性、非确定性值编码、消息延展性、哈希链延展性），但缺乏一个跨生态系统的统一框架来追踪其共同根源。

Method: 作者并未声称发现了新现象，而是做出三项贡献：(1) 按表示机制而非受影响系统进行系统化分类；(2) 将经典规范化安全（canonicalization security）与表示论和可计算理论基础联系起来；(3) 将其转化为可应用的审查程序——包括规范化义务、一系列审查步骤、字段类型分类和操作边界模型，以便预防性地识别风险。文章通过完整案例论证该类别，并与非表示问题的事故划清界限。

Result: 论文识别出所有零散的延展性/突变性漏洞均为同一违反唯一性条件的具体实例，沿两个基本方向：一个对象对应多个有效编码（对象侧多重表示）或一个编码对应多个对象（编码侧语义坍缩）。提供了可以预防性应用的结构化审查流程。

Conclusion: 作者有意将主张限制在可验证的范围：强制唯一性可以降低一类失败模式的可利用性，但它既不能替代其他防护措施，也不能对狭义上的密码学安全性作任何声明。论文旨在为跨生态系统的这一失败类别提供统一的系统化理解和预防性审查工具。

Abstract: Cryptographic systems operate on bytes but mean semantic objects. The translation between the two is rarely unique. Where this uniqueness is not enforced, an attack surface opens up as soon as a hash, a signature, replay protection, or consensus identity depends on the representation. The same class of failure has been discovered independently and named locally across many ecosystems, as transaction malleability, non-deterministic value encoding, message malleability, or hash chain malleability, without its common root being tracked as a cross-ecosystem grid. This work organizes the scattered findings systematically: it shows that they are instances of one violated uniqueness condition, along two basic directions: multiple valid codes for one object (object-side multiple representation) or one code for multiple objects (code-side semantic collapse). The contribution is explicitly not the discovery of the phenomenon, but is threefold: the systematization by representation mechanism rather than by affected system, the bridge between classical canonicalization security and a representation- and computability-theoretic foundation, and the translation into an applicable review procedure (a canonicalization obligation with a sequence of review steps, a field-type classification, and an operational boundary model) with which the risk can be recognized preventively. We substantiate the class with worked-out cases, delimit it against incidents that are not representation problems, and deliberately keep the claims to what is demonstrable: enforced uniqueness can reduce the exploitability of a failure class, but it neither replaces further protective measures or makes any statement about cryptographic security in the narrower sense

</details>


### [146] [Model Confidence Under Answer-Preserving Attacks: An Informativeness-Manipulability Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06571)
*Reza Khanmohammadi,Ivan Brugere,Simerjot Kaur,Charese H. Smiley,Kundan Thind,Mohammad M. Ghassemi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究视觉-语言系统置信度读数的鲁棒性，发现白盒、仅图像攻击在保持生成答案字节不变的情况下，可轻易破坏置信度作为监督信号的可信度。在四种VLM、三个VQA基准、五个置信度通道和两个防御估计器上，所有84个估计器-单元格组合均驳斥了均匀振幅证书；协调攻击使所有60个部署通道单元格的对抗判别力降至答案串先验(0.617)以下。置信度门控仿真中，转移攻击可使高达84.8%原本被拒绝的错误答案被接受，直接攻击下所有12个单元格在接受准确率上低于无门控基线。结论：在研究的威胁模型下，置信度是完整性敏感的监督信号，而非内在鲁棒信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署的视觉-语言系统常用置信度进行答案门控，因此置信度的鲁棒性对监督和治理至关重要。现有研究对图像扰动下置信度读数的安全性缺乏系统性验证，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在白盒、图像唯一攻击约束下（保持生成答案逐字节不变），作者建立了不可移动读数的理论下界（基于可达性假设的答案串准确率先验0.617）；设计直接和替代目标的攻击；引入均匀振幅证书作为可测量的防御保证；在四种VLM、三个VQA基准、五个置信度通道、两个防御估计器上系统评估；并扩展到隐藏状态干预、开放文本模型激活空间复制及置信度门控仿真。

Result: 直接或替代目标攻击在所有84个估计器-单元格组合中生成可行的逐项扰动，驳斥均匀振幅证书；协调的、正确性标签感知攻击使所有60个部署通道单元格（含59个起点高于先验的）的对抗判别力降至或低于答案串先验0.617；隐藏状态干预和激活空间复制表明置信度移动可在表征层面产生；四种防御族均未通过各自评估；门控仿真中转移攻击导致84.8%的被拒错误答案被接受，直接攻击下所有12个单元格接受准确率低于无门控基线。

Conclusion: 在所研究的威胁模型和预算下，置信度读数应被视为完整性敏感的监督信号，而非内在鲁棒的监督信号。置信度门控系统面对对抗性图像扰动时存在系统性脆弱性，且现有防御手段无法提供鲁棒替代方案。

Abstract: Deployed vision-language systems often gate their answers on confidence, making confidence robustness relevant to oversight. We study confidence readouts under white-box, image-only attacks constrained to preserve the generated answer byte-identically. Under a reachability assumption, an unmovable readout cannot outperform the answer-string accuracy prior, whose pooled value is 0.617. Independently of that assumption, a uniform amplitude certificate below a measurable threshold guarantees adversarial discrimination above the same floor. Across four vision-language models, three visual question answering benchmarks, five deployed confidence channels and two defense estimators, direct or surrogate-aimed attacks produce itemwise feasible perturbations that refute this uniform certificate in all 84 estimator-by-cell combinations. Coordinated correctness-label-aware attacks drive adversarial discrimination to or below the answer-string floor in all sixty deployed-channel cells, including all fifty-nine that begin above it. Hidden-state interventions and an open-ended text-model activation-space replication show that comparable confidence movement can be induced at the representation level rather than only through adversarial images. None of four tested defense families establishes a robust alternative under the specific evaluation applied to it. In a confidence-gated simulation, a coordinated token-probability attack transferred to a hidden-state gate causes up to 84.8% of previously rejected wrong answers to become accepted. After reweighting to each benchmark's natural correctness prevalence, accepted accuracy falls below the no-gate baseline in eight of twelve cells under transfer and all twelve under a direct gate-aimed attack. Under the studied threat model and budget, confidence is therefore an integrity-sensitive rather than intrinsically robust oversight signal.

</details>


### [147] [WhiteNet: Robust Identification of Overlapping IEEE 802.11 Signals Across Unseen Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06581)
*Ildi Alla,Vincent Lenders*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: WhiteNet是一个针对I/Q样本中信道变化问题的深度学习框架，通过物理启发的谱白化预处理抑制频率选择性衰落，并结合合成重叠混合器进行预训练，在不依赖大量真实多发射机数据的情况下，在公开的IEEE 802.11过空数据上显著缩小了未见信道条件导致的精度差距，同时参数数量比现有最优方法少7.7倍，并可蒸馏为紧凑变体用于边缘设备。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于I/Q样本训练的深度学习分类器在IEEE 802.11协议识别中表现出高精度，但当部署时的信道条件与训练时不同，性能会急剧下降。这种信道变异性问题严重限制了模型的实际部署，而获取覆盖多种信道条件的大量多发射机过空训练数据成本高昂。

Method: WhiteNet框架包含两个核心组件：(1) 谱白化（spectral whitening），一种基于物理原理的预处理步骤，用于抑制频率选择性衰落同时保留协议判别特征；(2) 合成重叠混合器（synthetic overlap mixer），模拟每个发射机的独立信道和共享接收机信号链，以生成合成训练数据，减少对真实多发射机过空采集的依赖。

Result: 在公开的过空IEEE 802.11数据上，WhiteNet显著缩小了未见信道条件导致的精度差距；相比现有最优方法，参数量减少7.7倍；此外可选择性蒸馏为紧凑变体，适用于功率受限的边缘设备进行粗粒度频谱感知。

Conclusion: WhiteNet通过物理启发的谱白化预处理和合成数据增强，有效解决了深度学习分类器在信道条件变化下的性能退化问题，在保持高精度的同时大幅降低模型复杂度，为边缘设备的实际部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning (DL) classifiers trained on I/Q samples achieve high accuracy for IEEE 802.11 protocol identification of overlapping signals, but their performance degrades sharply when channel conditions at deployment differ from those encountered during training. We present WhiteNet, a framework that addresses the problem of channel variability in I/Q samples. The central idea is spectral whitening, a physics-grounded preprocessing step that suppresses frequency-selective fading while preserving protocol-discriminative features. To reduce dependence on costly multi-transmitter over-the-air captures for training, we complement it with a synthetic overlap mixer featuring a physically accurate per-transmitter channel and shared-receiver signal chain for pre-training without extensive field data collection. On public over-the-air IEEE 802.11 data, WhiteNet closes a substantial portion of the accuracy gap caused by unseen channel conditions while using 7.7 times fewer parameters than the prior state of the art, and optionally distills to compact variants for coarse spectrum awareness on power-constrained edge devices.

</details>


### [148] [From Documentation to Zero-day Vulnerabilities: LLM-Driven Fuzzing of JavaScript Engines in PDF Readers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06641)
*Suyue Guo,Stijn Pletinckx,Tianle Yu,Yigitcan Kaya,Saad Ullah,Wenbo Guo,Christopher Kruegel,Giovanni Vigna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PDFuzzer是一个针对PDF引擎的新型模糊测试工具，利用大语言模型自动生成复杂且有意义的API调用序列，在三个主流PDF阅读器中比现有工具覆盖率提升高达48%，并发现31个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PDF阅读器模糊测试工具仅使用涉及单个API调用的简单测试用例，导致覆盖率有限，可能遗漏需要API调用序列才能触发的漏洞。

Method: PDFuzzer首先利用大语言模型（LLM）从JavaScript API手册和执行痕迹中提取规格，构建上下文无关文法并推断API调用之间的关系；然后基于这些文法和关系，使用约束求解器生成具体的API调用序列用于模糊测试。

Result: 在Adobe Acrobat Reader、Foxit PDF Reader和PDF-XChange Editor三个主流PDF阅读器上，PDFuzzer相比现有工具（TypeOracle、Favocado、Cooper）和LLM模糊器（Fuzz4All、naive LLM）覆盖率最高提升48%，发现31个零日漏洞（从信息泄露到任意代码执行），各阶段LLM准确率达93-98%。漏洞已通过协调披露流程报告给厂商并获得漏洞赏金。

Conclusion: 通过将LLM、文法推断和约束求解相结合来自动生成API调用序列，可以显著提升PDF引擎模糊测试的覆盖率和漏洞发现能力，且每个组件（特别是LLM）的必要性已通过消融实验验证。

Abstract: Existing fuzzers for PDF readers rely on simple test cases that involve only individual API calls, leading to limited coverage and potentially missing vulnerabilities that require sequences of API calls. To address these limitations, we propose PDFuzzer, a novel PDF engine fuzzer that automatically generates complex and meaningful API call sequences. PDFuzzer first uses a Large Language Model (LLM) to construct context-free grammars and infer the relationships between individual API calls from specifications extracted from JavaScript API manuals and execution traces. Based on the grammars and relationships, PDFuzzer employs a constraint solver to generate concrete API call sequences for fuzzing. Our experiments show that PDFuzzer significantly outperforms state-of-the-art PDF fuzzers (TypeOracle, Favocado, and Cooper) and LLM-based fuzzers (Fuzz4All, naive LLM) on three mainstream PDF readers: Adobe Acrobat Reader, Foxit PDF Reader, and PDF-XChange Editor. PDFuzzer achieves up to 48% higher coverage than existing tools and identifies 31 zero-day vulnerabilities in these readers, from information leakage to arbitrary code execution. Our ablation study validates the necessity of each component, including LLMs, which achieve high accuracy across all pipeline stages (93-98%). We disclosed all vulnerabilities to the vendors via a coordinated vulnerability disclosure process and received bug bounties.

</details>


### [149] [CyberLLM: A Multi-Agent LLM Framework for Autonomous Detection and Guarded Response in Automotive Cybersecurity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06651)
*Nenad Petrovic,Oussama Jeddou,Feres Ben Fraj,Vahid Zolfaghari,Fengjunjie Pan,Andre Schamschurko,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CyberLLM是一个多智能体、LLM编排的框架，用于软件定义汽车（SDV）的自主漏洞检测与修复。它结合确定性检测层（正则、AST分析器、拓扑图检查）与LLM推理精化，在形式化运行时安全护栏下执行操作。在9个真实ECU模块、47个分层漏洞的基准上，确定性层达到34%覆盖率（完全精确），加入LLM精化后将覆盖率提升至约70%（F1=0.83），且对干净对照组零误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件定义汽车（SDV）大幅扩展了攻击面（源代码、运行时日志、部署拓扑），但安全约束禁止自主代理在无监督下行动。如何在保证安全的约束下实现LLM智能体的自主网络防御，是一个关键挑战。

Method: CyberLLM采用多智能体架构：1）确定性层（正则规则、AST分析器、拓扑图检查）提供高召回下限；2）LLM精化与完整性检查提升精度；3）决策智能体聚合发现、按人类资产分类法标注、选择分级响应，并通过签名的跨会话记忆和重新规划反馈提升信心；4）每个动作必须通过四个上下文安全属性和独立的动作对齐预言机验证，拒绝的动作触发升级和重新规划；5）对称攻击流水线生成并重放漏洞利用以测试双端。

Result: 在独立、有ground truth的基准上（9个原始汽车ECU模块，C/C++/Rust语言，47个分层漏洞加干净对照组），确定性层覆盖34%标注漏洞且精度完美；加入LLM精化和完整性检查后覆盖率达约70%，F1=0.83，对干净对照组零误报。

Conclusion: 结果表明，当LLM智能体被包裹在确定性、可审计的安全信封中时，它们能够执行有用的自主网络防御。这验证了"LLM+形式化安全护栏"的结合范式在汽车网络安全领域的可行性。

Abstract: Software-Defined Vehicles (SDVs) expand the automotive attack surface across source code, runtime logs, and deployment topologies, while safety constraints forbid autonomous agents from acting without oversight. This paper presents CyberLLM, a multi-agent, LLM-orchestrated framework that autonomously detects vulnerabilities and executes remediations under a formal, runtime safety guard. Detection combines a deterministic layer (regex rules, AST analyzers, and topology graph checks) with an LLM refinement pass, so a high-recall floor is complemented by high-precision reasoning. A decision agent aggregates findings, tags them with a human-centric asset taxonomy, and selects a tiered response, ratcheting its confidence with signed cross-session memory and re-planning feedback. Every action is validated against four contextual security properties and an independent action-alignment oracle before it is allowed to run, and refused actions trigger escalation and re-planning. A symmetric attack pipeline generates and replays exploits so both sides can be exercised on the same scenarios. On an independent, ground-truthed benchmark of nine original automotive ECU modules in C, C++, and Rust seeding 47 layered vulnerabilities plus clean controls, the always-on deterministic layer covers 34\% of the labeled vulnerabilities at perfect precision, and adding the grounded LLM refinement and completeness passes roughly doubles coverage to about 70\% (F1 $0.83$) while producing zero false positives on the clean controls. The results indicate that LLM agents can perform useful autonomous cyber-defense when wrapped in a deterministic, auditable safety envelope.

</details>


### [150] [Policy-Masked Private Experts: Auditable and Reversible Capability Access Control in Sparse MoE Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06690)
*Zhuoheng Huang,Mukesh Singh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"策略掩码私有专家"（Policy-Masked Private Experts）机制：冻结预训练稀疏MoE模型，训练独立专家分支，在top-k路由前根据授权决定选择公共池还是私有池，从而实现对"哪些新训练参数可被前向传播到达"的执行级访问控制。实验表明在声明的TCB下未授权请求零私有专家执行，且私有分支能显著提升工具使用等任务能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型访问控制机制通常只规范行为层面的输出限制，但底层计算对每个请求完全相同。作者提出一个不同的系统问题：受信任的授权机制能否决定哪些新训练参数可以被前向传播到达？从而在参数路径层面实现细粒度的执行隔离。

Method: 方法包括：(1) 冻结预训练稀疏MoE模型，训练一个与原有专家不相交的新专家分支；(2) 在top-k路由之前根据授权状态在公共专家池和私有专家池之间进行选择；(3) 在Qwen3-30B-A3B和DeepSeek-V2-Lite上验证。私有分支采用BF16精度、更新全部32个私有专家，公共指纹保持不变。对比方法包括参数匹配的LoRA和后验请求gate机制。

Result: 在64个对抗场景和96个deny/fail-closed事件中，未授权私有执行次数为零；独立hooks精确匹配11,616条路由的私有行且allow-deny-allow恢复完全精确。Qwen上私有分支在工具使用上提升5.0pp（p=0.03125）和21.3pp（95% CI [13.3,29.3]，p=0.000031）；三盲模型评估者确认18.7pp外部效应（95% CI [9.3,28.0]）。LoRA在post-hoc gate下仍有1,225个适配器调用处于deny状态，而私有专家分支为零。DeepSeek复现路由不变性并获27.0pp增益。密封评估近似中性。

Conclusion: 该工作支持对训练参数路径进行可审计、可逆的执行控制，表明执行控制与任务效用可以分离。但有用迁移仍然依赖数据分布，密封评估的近中性结果提示性能提升具有分布依赖性，私有分支的能力转移并非无条件成立。

Abstract: Most language-model access controls regulate behavior while leaving the same computation available to every request. We study a different systems question: can trusted authorization determine which newly trained parameters are reachable by the forward pass? Policy-Masked Private Experts freezes a pretrained sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) model, trains a disjoint expert branch, and selects the public or private pool before top-k routing. The resulting claim is narrow but testable: under the declared trusted computing base (TCB), an unauthorized request executes no private expert. It does not imply that the public model lacks the same semantic capability.
  We test this separation between execution control and task utility in Qwen3-30B-A3B and DeepSeek-V2-Lite. Three Qwen BF16 seeds update all 32 private experts while the public fingerprint remains unchanged. Across 64 adversarial scenarios and 96 deny/fail-closed events, unauthorized private execution is zero; independent hooks exactly match 11,616 routed private rows and allow-deny-allow recovery is exact. On two prospectively frozen Qwen benchmarks, the private branch improves exact tool use by 5.0 percentage points (pp) (five versus zero discordances; one-sided Holm p = 0.03125, corresponding two-sided exact p = 0.0625) and 21.3 pp (percentile-bootstrap 95% CI [13.3, 29.3], Holm p = 0.000031). Three arm-blinded model evaluators retain a positive external effect of 18.7 pp (95% CI [9.3, 28.0]). A parameter-matched Lora has similar external utility, but a post-hoc request gate leaves 1,225 adapter calls under deny; the disjoint expert branch leaves none. DeepSeek reproduces the route invariant and gains 27.0 pp. A valid sealed evaluation is near-neutral. These results support auditable, reversible control over a trained parameter path, while showing that useful transfer remains distribution dependent.

</details>


### [151] [Tight Security for BBS Signatures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06724)
*Rutchathon Chairattana-Apirom,Dennis Hofheinz,Stefano Tessaro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示了BBS签名方案相对于q-SDH假设的紧致安全性鸿沟的本质：当每条消息最多签名一次（覆盖去随机化签名的常见实际场景）时，可证明紧致安全归约；但当同一消息可被多次签名时，通过元归约论证证明不存在紧致的代数归约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BBS签名是隐私保护认证系统的底层代数构造，正在被W3C和IRTF标准化。现有BBS安全证明（Tessaro和Zhu, EUROCRYPT '23）在q-SDH假设下不紧致，而紧致证明仅存在于效率较低的BBS+变体中（Schäge, JoC '15）。理解这一紧致性鸿沟是否固有，对于推动标准化和工业界采用至关重要，且近期密码分析（Chairattana-Apirom和Tessaro, ASIACRYPT '25）显示紧致归约到q-SDH已是最佳可能。

Method: 论文采用双管齐下的方法：(1) 正向结果：针对每条消息最多签名一次的场景（涵盖去随机化签名的实际用途），构造一个新的紧致归约；(2) 负向结果：运用元归约（meta-reduction）论证技术，证明若允许对同一消息生成多个签名，则不存在任何代数归约能够紧致地归约到q-SDH（及其变体）假设。

Result: 成功实现了两个方向的结论：在受限场景下给出了BBS的紧致安全证明，同时通过元归约证明了在一般场景下紧致代数归约的不可能性，从而完整刻画了该紧致性鸿沟的边界。

Conclusion: BBS签名的紧致安全性取决于签名使用模式：消息唯一签名场景下紧致安全是可达的，而多签名场景下紧致代数归约到q-SDH在本质上是不可能的。这一结论为标准化工作和工业实践提供了明确的安全指导。

Abstract: This paper studies the concrete security of BBS signatures (Boneh, Boyen, Shacham, CRYPTO '04; Camenisch and Lysyanskaya, CRYPTO '04), a popular algebraic construction of digital signatures which underlies practical privacy-preserving authentication systems and is undergoing standardization by the W3C and IRTF.
  Schäge (Journal of Cryptology '15) gave a tight standard-model security proof under the q-SDH assumption for a less efficient variant of the scheme, called BBS+--here, q is the number of issued signatures. In contrast, the security proof for BBS (Tessaro and Zhu, EUROCRYPT '23), also under the q-SDH assumption, is \emph{not} tight. Nonetheless, this recent proof shifted both standardization and industry adoption towards the more efficient BBS, instead of BBS+, and for this reason, it is important to understand whether this tightness gap is inherent. Recent cryptanalysis by Chairattana-Apirom and Tessaro (ASIACRYPT '25) also shows that a tight reduction to q-SDH is the best we can hope for.
  This paper closes this gap in two different ways. On the positive end, we show a novel tight reduction for BBS in the case where each message is signed at most once--this case covers in particular the common practical use case which derandomizes signing. On the negative end, we use a meta-reduction argument to prove that if we allow generating multiple signatures for the same message, then {\em no} algebraic reduction to q-SDH (and its variants) can be tight.

</details>


### [152] [Retrieval-Constrained Policy Optimization for Attack Technique Extraction from Cyber Threat Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06778)
*Jiayun Zhang,Junshen Xu,Zejun Xie,Yi Fan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了TTP-R1，一个两阶段框架，结合检索增强的监督微调（SFT）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），用于将网络威胁情报（CTI）文本自动映射到MITRE ATT&CK技术。通过混合检索器缩小标签空间，再使用GRPO策略优化从预测技术集合的精度、召回率和输出格式三个维度进行直接监督，在四个CTI基准上取得最优平均F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工标注CTI文本到MITRE ATT&CK技术映射成本高昂且无法扩展；现有自动化方法各有缺陷：多标签分类器难以处理严重的类别不平衡和大标签空间，基于LLM的方法（检索流水线和微调生成器）将技术标注视为序列生成而非集合预测，缺少对预测技术集合正确性和完整性的直接监督。

Method: TTP-R1采用两阶段框架：第一阶段使用混合检索器（hybrid retriever）将大规模标签空间缩减为候选集，并对LLM进行检索增强的监督微调（SFT），使其学会从候选中选择正确技术；第二阶段应用Group Relative Policy Optimization（GRPO）配合分解奖励（decomposed reward），该奖励直接监督预测技术集合的精度（precision）、召回率（recall）和输出格式。

Result: 在四个CTI基准数据集上，TTP-R1取得最佳平均F1值。相比使用检索增强的Claude Sonnet 4.5，子技术级别F1提升了7.4个百分点；作为8B参数模型在单GPU上服务时，运行速度快28倍。

Conclusion: 将技术标注问题重新定义为集合预测而非序列生成，通过强化学习直接优化集合层面的精度、召回率和格式，可以在显著提升性能的同时大幅降低推理成本，证明了该方法的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Mapping cyber threat intelligence (CTI) text to MITRE ATT&CK techniques is essential for structured threat analysis, yet manual annotation is costly and does not scale. The ATT&CK taxonomy comprises several hundred attack techniques, and a single CTI passage may describe multiple techniques, making accurate and complete extraction challenging. Existing automated approaches fall short in different ways: multi-label classifiers struggle with severe class imbalance and the large label space, while LLM-based methods--retrieval pipelines and fine-tuned generators--optimize token-level objectives that treat technique annotation as sequence generation rather than set prediction, lacking direct supervision on whether the predicted technique set is correct and complete. We propose TTP-R1, a two-stage framework that combines retrieval-augmented supervised fine-tuning (SFT) with reinforcement learning using verifiable rewards (RLVR). A hybrid retriever first narrows the large label space to a candidate set, and a fine-tuned LLM learns to select the correct techniques. We then apply Group Relative Policy Optimization with a decomposed reward that directly supervises the precision, recall, and output format of the predicted technique set. Across four CTI benchmarks, TTP-R1 achieves the best average F1, improving sub-technique-level F1 by 7.4 percentage points over Claude Sonnet 4.5 with retrieval augmentation, while running 28x faster when served as an 8B-parameter model on a single GPU.

</details>


### [153] [LoRAScan: Detecting Backdoor Prompts in Low-Rank Adapters for Large Language Models via Down-Projection Activation Spikes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06795)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: LoRAScan是首个适配器感知的防御方法，在推理时检测并拒绝包含触发器的恶意输入，无需修改LoRA适配器参数。它利用约5%的LoRA插入位点在干净输入中保持稳定、但在触发器存在时出现高度集中的下投影激活尖峰这一关键观察，在部署前识别这些低方差插入位点并在推理时监控，实现对约98.49%恶意输入的拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA使大型语言模型能够通过紧凑适配器实现高效专精化和分发，但不受信任的适配器引入了供应链威胁：带后门的适配器可在输入包含隐藏触发器时让模型生成有害内容、恶意代码、政治宣传或隐蔽广告。现有防御存在不足：适配器无关的防御合并适配器与基础模型会稀释后门信号、降低检测性能；适配器感知方法要么训练防御性适配器修复后门基础模型（解决的是反向问题而非保护适配器本身），要么依赖分类器将整个适配器标记为可疑并需要单独缓解。这些方法都忽视了带后门适配器中触发器输入产生的独特潜在空间签名。

Method: LoRAScan提出以下方法：1) 核心观察——约5%的LoRA插入位点在干净输入中保持低方差稳定，但当输入包含触发器时，这些位点的LoRA下投影激活会出现高度集中的尖峰。2) 部署前阶段：识别这些低方差插入位点。3) 推理阶段：仅监控这些选定的插入位点，检测是否存在触发器导致的异常激活模式，从而拒绝恶意输入，且无需修改适配器参数。

Result: 在标准LLM后门基准测试中，LoRAScan能拒绝约98.49%的恶意输入，同时在干净输入上保持较低的误报率，并在多种评估设置下优于现有防御方法。

Conclusion: LoRAScan通过利用LoRA插入位点的激活签名差异，首次实现了无需修改适配器参数的推理时检测与拒绝机制，为LoRA适配器的供应链安全提供了有效的防御方案。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) enables efficient specialization and distribution of large language models through compact adapters. However, untrusted adapters introduce a supply-chain threat: a backdoored adapter can cause a model to generate harmful content, malicious code, political propaganda, or covert advertisements when an input contains a hidden trigger. Adapter-agnostic defenses merge the adapter with the base model, which dilutes backdoor signals and reduces detection performance. Existing adapter-aware methods do not address how to safely use a potentially backdoored adapter. Instead, they either train a defensive adapter to repair a backdoored base model, addressing the inverse problem rather than securing the adapter itself, or rely on a classifier that flags the entire adapter as suspicious and requires separate mitigation. These methods overlook the distinct latent-space signatures produced by trigger-bearing inputs in backdoored adapters.
  We introduce LoRAScan, the first adapter-aware defense that detects and rejects trigger-bearing inputs at inference time without modifying adapter parameters. Our key observation is that a small subset of LoRA insertion sites, approximately 5%, remains stable across clean inputs but exhibits highly concentrated spikes in LoRA down-projection activations when a trigger is present. LoRAScan identifies these low-variance insertion sites before model deployment and monitors them during inference. Across standard LLM backdoor benchmarks, LoRAScan rejects approximately 98.49 of malicious inputs with a small error rate on clean inputs, outperforming existing defenses across diverse evaluation settings.

</details>


### [154] [On a General Theoretical Framework for Radio Frequency Fingerprint-Based Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06805)
*Yuanyu Zhang,Jianing Wang,Shuangrui Zhao,Pinchang Zhang,Yulong Shen,Xiaohong Jiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个通用的理论框架，将射频指纹（RFF）建模与身份认证属性分析相结合，从理论上解释RFF为何及如何作为可靠的设备身份，并基于该理论讨论了通信与认证协同设计的议题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然基于RFF的无线设备认证已在不同数据集和场景中广泛研究，但缺乏基础理论来解释RFF为何及如何能作为可靠的设备身份，这严重阻碍了该认证技术的实际应用。

Method: 作者提出了一个两阶段的理论框架：首先进行RFF建模，揭示RFF沿发射机-信道-接收机链路如何被诱导、演化和观测；随后进行认证属性分析，从唯一性、稳定性、可区分性和不可伪造性四个维度考察RFF的可信度。框架将RFF的形成/演化过程与这些认证属性相连接。

Result: 该框架为理解基于RFF的认证在实际中为何及如何可信提供了坚实基础，并基于框架的理论洞察讨论了通信与认证的协同设计问题。

Conclusion: 通过将RFF的建模与认证属性分析整合进一个统一框架，本文为RFF认证提供了理论基础，弥补了该领域缺乏理论指导的空白，并为未来通信-认证协同设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: While radio frequency fingerprint (RFF)-based wireless device authentication has been widely studied across different datasets and scenarios, there still lacks a fundamental theory to explain why and how RFF can serve as a reliable device identity, significantly hindering the practical application of such an authentication technology. In this article, we integrate the RFF modeling with authentication property analysis to propose a general theoretical framework to facilitate the development of such a theory. The RFF modeling process reveals how RFFs are induced, evolved and observed along the transmitter-channel-receiver chain, built upon which, the authentication property analysis process then outlines how the trustworthiness of an RFF should be examined in terms of its uniqueness, stability, distinguishability, and unforgeability. By linking the RFF formation/evolution to these authentication properties, the framework offers a solid foundation for understanding why and how RFF-based authentication is trustworthy in practice. We also discuss the communication-authentication co-design issue based on the theoretical insights from the proposed framework.

</details>


### [155] [POKEx: Performance analysis of POKE-key exchange and SIDH-variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06826)
*Hyeonhak Kim,Suhri Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对基于POKE的密钥交换与多种SIDH变体进行了性能对比分析。在NIST安全级别1下，POKE-based KEM比terSIDH快约21.21倍，比CSIDH快约64.97倍，是目前最有前景的同源密码密钥交换方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIDH曾是同源密码学（isogeny-based）领域最具代表性的算法，入选NIST PQC第4轮备选方案。然而2022年Castryck和Decru提出的密钥恢复攻击证明了SIDH在经典计算机上存在多项式时间攻击，使其安全性崩溃并被移除。尽管提出了MSIDH、MD-SIDH、bin/terSIDH等对策，但这些方案仍面临性能瓶颈。因此需要寻找既有安全性保障又具有高效性能的同源密码方案。

Method: 本文将POKE协议（Basso和Maino在Eurocrypt 2025提出）适配为密钥交换算法，并针对NIST安全级别1，将此POKE-based KEM与M-SIDH、terSIDH和CSIDH进行基准性能对比测试。POKE的核心思想是将SIDH类协议与高维（higher-dimensional）同源结合，通过增加维度来抵消对SIDH的攻击威胁。

Result: 实验结果表明，在NIST安全级别1下，POKE-based KEM比terSIDH快约21.21倍，比CSIDH快约64.97倍。这显示了POKE在同源密码密钥交换领域中的显著性能优势。

Conclusion: POKE-based KEM是目前最有前景的同源密码密钥交换候选方案，它在保持对SIDH类攻击抵抗能力的同时，实现了远超现有同类方案（terSIDH、CSIDH）的性能表现。这一结果表明POKE有望成为后量子密码学中同源密码路线的中坚力量。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a comparative performance analysis of the POKE-based key exchange and SIDH variants. SIDH gained attention for its small key size and efficient performance, and has been selected as an alternate candidate in NIST PQC Round 4. However, following the key recovery attack by Castryck and Decru in 2022, SIDH was shown to be vulnerable to polynomial-time attacks on classical computers, undermining its security and removing it from consideration. Given that SIDH was regarded as the leading isogeny-based algorithm, several countermeasures, such as MSIDH, MD-SIDH, and bin/terSIDH have been proposed. However, these approaches still face performance limitations. Meanwhile, POKE, proposed by Basso and Maino at Eurocrypt 2025, is an isogeny-based public key encryption scheme that combines a SIDH-like protocol with higher-dimensional isogenies and has drawn attention for its efficient performance. In this work, we adapt POKE into a key exchange algorithm and benchmark it against M-SIDH, terSIDH, and CSIDH. Targeting NIST security level 1, POKE-based KEM is approximately 21.21 times faster than terSIDH and 64.97 times faster than CSIDH, showing that the POKE-based KEM is currently the most promising isogeny-based key exchange candidate.

</details>


### [156] [MIFA: An MILP-based Framework for Improving Differential Fault Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06837)
*Hanbeom Shin,Insung Kim,Sunyeop Kim,Byoungjin Seok,Deukjo Hong,Jaechul Sung,Seokhie Hong,Sangjin Lee,Dongjae Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解器的新型差分故障分析（DFA）框架，可攻击更深轮数并减少所需故障注入次数。通过系统分析所有可能单比特翻转故障的输入差分，确定最有效的故障注入比特位置，并理论上计算所需故障数量。对DEFAULT密码，仅需在倒数第六轮注入3个故障或倒数第七、八轮各注入2个故障即可将密钥空间降至1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DFA攻击一直是分组密码分析的重要方向。DEFAULT密码声称具有64位DFA安全性，但此前的工作（如Nageler等人的信息组合技术以及Jana等人的单解差分路径搜索）已被证明可以攻破，但所需故障注入次数仍然较多，且攻击深度有限。作者希望能开发一种更高效的DFA框架，能够在更深的轮次进行攻击，同时减少故障注入数量。

Method: 提出一个利用混合整数线性规划（MILP）求解器的DFA框架，能够攻击比以往更深轮次。同时，提出一种系统化方法，通过分析所有可能的单比特翻转故障对应的输入差分，来确定最有效的故障注入比特位置，从而进一步减少所需故障数量，并能从理论上计算所需的故障注入次数。

Result: 对于DEFAULT密码，在倒数第六轮注入3个故障，或在倒数第七轮和第八轮各注入2个故障，即可将密钥空间降至1。相比先前工作（Jana等人在倒数第五轮需要5个故障），新框架显著减少了故障注入次数并扩展了可攻击的深度。

Conclusion: 所提出的MILP-based DFA框架在攻击深度和故障注入效率上均优于已有方法，且系统性分析故障位置的方法使故障数量的理论计算成为可能，为DFA攻击提供了更加系统化和高效的方案。

Abstract: At ASIACRYPT 2021, Baksi et al. introduced DEFAULT, a block cipher designed to algorithmically resist Differential Fault Attack (DFA), claiming 64-bit DFA security regardless of the number of injected faults. At EUROCRYPT 2022, Nageler et al. demonstrated that DEFAULT's claimed DFA resistance can be broken by applying an information-combining technique. More recently, at ASIACRYPT 2024, Jana et al. improved DFA by searching for differential trails with a single solution. They showed that, for DEFAULT with a simple key schedule, injecting five faults at the fifth-to-last round reduces the key space to one, and for BAKSHEESH, injecting twelve faults at the third-to-last round achieves the same result. In this paper, we propose a new DFA framework that utilizes a Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) solver. This framework makes it possible to attack deeper rounds than previously achieved, reducing the number of fault injections required for key recovery. Furthermore, we present a method to determine the most efficient fault injection bit positions by systematically analyzing the input differences from all possible single bit-flip faults, thereby further reducing the required number of faults. This systematic analysis has the significant advantage of allowing us to theoretically calculate the required number of faults. Applying our framework, for DEFAULT, injecting three faults at the sixth-to-last round and two faults at the seventh- and eighth-to-last rounds reduces the key space to one.

</details>


### [157] [Understanding and Improving Model Editing for Secure Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06848)
*Weifeng Sun,Quanjun Zhang,Yuchen Chen,Chengran Yang,Gou Tan,David Lo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了模型编辑作为代码生成安全的模型级加固机制，提出SafeEdit后编辑精炼方法，在保持安全性的同时改善功能正确性，并与推理时方法CoSec互补结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成代码时可能复现训练数据中的不安全实现。现有研究主要聚焦推理时加固（如CoSec），它不修改目标模型但依赖辅助组件且增加运行时开销。需要探索模型级加固机制，即模型编辑，来从根本上提升代码生成安全性。

Method: 系统评估3种SOTA模型编辑方法在多种LLM家族上的安全性、鲁棒性、泛化性和功能正确性表现，与推理时方法CoSec对比。提出SafeEdit，一种后编辑精炼方法，结合功能微调与编辑感知正则化，缓解模型编辑导致的功能正确性下降问题。并评估SafeEdit与CoSec的互补组合效果。

Result: 模型编辑在已知漏洞类型上比CoSec获得更大安全增益（较原始模型提升15%-25%），且在提示扰动下保持稳定；但对未知漏洞的迁移不可靠且可能降低功能正确性。SafeEdit在8个目标LLM上，T=0.1/0.4/0.8时相对UltraEdit的Pass@1分别提升11.73/13.70/15.50个百分点，同时基本保持安全性；相对CoSec实现7.54%-12.04%的安全比率增益。CodeGuard+上的评估确认了联合安全与正确生成能力的提升。

Conclusion: 模型编辑可作为安全代码生成的有效模型级加固机制，SafeEdit有效缓解了其安全与功能正确性之间的权衡，且与推理时方法CoSec互补，组合使用可进一步提升安全性同时保持强功能正确性，为模型编辑在安全代码生成中的应用提供了有力的实证指导。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used for code generation, yet they can reproduce vulnerable implementations learned from insecure training patterns. Prior work has mainly explored inference-time hardening, which reduces insecure generations without modifying the target model but relies on auxiliary components and adds runtime overhead. We conduct the first systematic study of model editing as a model-level hardening mechanism for secure code generation. We evaluate 3 state-of-the-art editing methods across diverse LLM families and compare them with CoSec, a representative inference-time approach, focusing on security, robustness, generalization, and functional correctness. Model editing yields larger security gains than CoSec on seen vulnerability types, improving security ratios by 15%-25% over vanilla models, with gains remaining stable under prompt perturbations. However, these improvements transfer unreliably to unseen vulnerabilities and can reduce functional correctness. To mitigate this trade-off, we propose SafeEdit, a post-edit refinement method combining functional tuning with edit-aware regularization. Across eight target LLMs, SafeEdit improves Pass@1 over UltraEdit by 11.73/13.70/15.50 percentage points at T=0.1/0.4/0.8 while largely preserving security. Compared with CoSec, it achieves relative security-ratio gains of 7.54%-12.04%. Additional evaluation on CodeGuard+ confirms improved joint secure-and-correct generation. SafeEdit and CoSec are also complementary, and their combination can further improve security while maintaining strong functional correctness. Overall, our results provide evidence-backed guidance for applying model editing to secure code generation.

</details>


### [158] [SynChain: Inducing Computer-Use Agent Systems to Construct Their Own Attack Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06862)
*Fuyao Zhang,Jiaming Zhang,Che Wang,Boyang Chen,Yurong Hao,Xiongtao Sun,Guowei Guan,Blaise Delattre,Yang Cao,Wei Yang Bryan Lim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一种针对计算机使用智能体（CUA）的新型攻击范式SynChain，即恶意影响可被隐蔽地嵌入智能体自主合成的工件（如技能和记忆）的结构冗余中，使其存活于内部状态更新并绕过标准审查，通过持久化感知的有监督微调诱导智能体生成看似良性实则带毒的工件，从而使休眠载荷在未来工作流中以可信上下文重新激活，全过程无需新的恶意外部输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全防御大多将攻击视为外部触发或时间受限的，未能解决攻击如何通过智能体自身的持久状态在内部传播这一关键漏洞。随着CUAs将大语言模型转变为能够生成、存储和复用工件（技能、记忆条目）的持久执行系统，这种内部传播风险变得更加严峻。

Method: 提出SynChain自合成攻击范式，利用持久化感知的方向性监督微调（persistence-aware directed supervised fine-tuning）诱导智能体创建带毒但看起来良性的工件。同时构建CUAChain数据集，包含30条良性任务链和三种攻击目标，用于系统评估攻击传播效果。

Result: 在OpenClaw、Codex和Claude Code三种系统上，在四种防御设置下进行大量实验，SynChain均实现了较高的攻击成功率，并优于适配的基线方法。

Conclusion: 保障CUAs的安全需要具备跨任务执行轨迹的溯源感知推理能力（provenance-aware reasoning），仅靠现有防御措施不足以应对通过持久状态内部传播的隐蔽攻击。

Abstract: Computer-use agents~(CUAs) have transformed large language models into persistent execution systems capable of generating, storing, and reusing artifacts like skills and memory entries. However, existing security defenses largely treat attacks as externally triggered or temporally bounded, leaving a critical gap in addressing how compromise can propagate internally through an agent's own persistent state. We reveal that malicious influence can be covertly embedded into the structural redundancies of autonomously synthesized artifacts, allowing it to survive internal state updates and bypass standard vetting mechanisms. To formalize this threat, we introduce SynChain, a self-synthesized attack paradigm utilizing persistence-aware directed supervised fine-tuning to induce agents to create poisoned yet benign-looking artifacts. To systematically evaluate this propagation, we construct CUAChain, a dataset comprising 30 benign task chains and three attack objectives. SynChain enables dormant payloads to seamlessly reactivate in future workflows as trusted context, operating entirely without new malicious exogenous inputs. Extensive experiments on OpenClaw, Codex, and Claude Code under four defense settings demonstrate that SynChain achieves high attack success and outperforms adapted baselines, proving that securing CUAs requires provenance-aware reasoning over cross-task execution trajectories.

</details>


### [159] [Rigid-Covert GNSS Spoofing of UAV Swarms: A Structural Blind Spot, Its Detection Limit, and Absolute-Anchor Defenses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06885)
*Minseok Park,Joon Soo Yoo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了无人机群协同防御中基于相对几何（无人机间距离）校验GNSS位置存在结构性盲区：一种常见的慢变平移（RigidShift）保持所有两两距离不变，任何仅依赖相对测量的检测器都无法察觉（规范自由论证）。作者在距离验证和半定可行性基线上验证了该盲区，推导了锚点残差检测器的漂移相关检测下限，并提出一个中心化锚定恢复流程，通过Byzantine鲁棒拟合重建被攻击无人机群的绝对位置。在统计仿真、ArduPilot软件在环和Gazebo含渲染视觉锚点的实验中，该方法在约10.1m GNSS漂移下将非锚定无人机位置恢复至0.39m中位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机群通常依赖GNSS定位，但GNSS易受欺骗攻击。常见的防御策略是将GNSS位置与无人机间的相对几何测量进行交叉校验。然而，作者发现这种相对几何通道存在根本性的规范自由（gauge freedom）问题：所有无人机同时发生一个共同的慢变平移时，所有两两距离保持不变，导致任何仅依赖相对测量的检测算法都无法察觉此类攻击。这一结构性盲区意味着现有防御体系存在根本漏洞，亟需揭示并量化其影响，以及探索如何借助外部参考恢复可观测性。

Method: 作者首先通过规范自由论证证明RigidShift对任何相对测量检测器不可观测，并在距离验证和半定可行性基线上实证验证。其次，推导了校准锚点残差检测器的漂移相关检测下限2γ/(1-t_s/T)，并识别出与理论预测高度吻合（斜率2.66 vs 2.67）的检测器特定噪声下限。随后提出中心化锚定恢复管道：(1)从机间距离重建机群几何；(2)通过Byzantine鲁棒拟合对齐到受信任锚点子集；(3)恢复非锚定无人机的绝对位置。为应对无清洁时段标注的情况，提出分段估计器联合估计锚点漂移、攻击率和起始时间。还分析了非共线锚点几何、锚点覆盖、τ→0漂移攻击混叠及多数锚点被攻陷等限制条件。

Result: 在统计仿真中，该方法在约10.1m GNSS漂移下将非锚定无人机位置恢复至0.39m中位误差（20个随机种子）；在含渲染视觉锚点的多SITL（软件在环）实验中达到7.1cm误差（5个种子）。检测下限的理论推导与实验观测高度一致。

Conclusion: 仅依赖无人机间相对几何的防御机制存在固有的RigidShift盲区，无法单独防御共同慢变平移型GNSS欺骗攻击。但通过引入受信任的锚点参考并结合Byzantine鲁棒拟合，可以有效恢复被欺骗机群的绝对位置（亚米级精度）。该工作明确了外部参考在恢复可观测性中的必要性，并系统刻画了锚点几何、覆盖率和攻击时序等因素对恢复精度的边界限制。所有评估基于仿真，尚未涉及RF欺骗硬件或物理机群实验。

Abstract: Cooperative UAV-swarm defenses commonly cross-check GNSS positions against measured inter-drone geometry. We show that this relative-geometry channel has a structural blind spot: a common, slowly varying translation (a rigid-covert shift, RigidShift) preserves all pairwise distances and is therefore unobservable to any relative-only detector (a gauge-freedom argument). We validate this blindness on distance-verification and semidefinite-feasibility baselines, while explicitly distinguishing it from onboard inertial/GNSS monitors that can raise a bare alarm but cannot recover the swarm's true position. To quantify when an external reference restores observability, we derive the drift-dependent detection floor $2γ/(1-t_s/T)$ for a calibrated anchor-residual detector and empirically identify an additional detector-specific noise floor (measured slope 2.66 vs. predicted 2.67). We then present a centralized anchor-rooted recovery pipeline that reconstructs swarm geometry from inter-drone ranges, aligns it to a trusted-anchor subset with Byzantine-robust fitting, and recovers the absolute positions of non-anchored drones. A segmented estimator jointly estimates anchor drift, attack rate, and onset when no clean-epoch label is available. Across statistical simulations, ArduPilot software-in-the-loop experiments, and Gazebo experiments with rendered vision anchors, the method recovers the positions of non-anchored drones to a median error of 0.39 m (20 seeds) under approximately 10.1 m of GNSS drift, and to 7.1 cm (5 seeds) in the rendered-vision multi-SITL setting. We also characterize the explicit limits imposed by non-collinear anchor geometry, anchor coverage, $τ\to0$ drift-attack aliasing, and majority anchor compromise. All evaluations are simulation-based and use no RF spoofing hardware or physical swarm.

</details>


### [160] [When Context Bites: Detecting RAG Poisoning via Document-Level Attention Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06947)
*Yingtao Ren,Ziyi Zhao,Yiwei Fu,Xiao Luo,Yu-Cheng Chang,Chin-Teng Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出D-SCAN检测框架，通过分析生成器内部的注意力动态（Attention Collapse现象）来识别RAG系统中的投毒攻击，克服了传统基于困惑度和一致性检查方法的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统容易受到投毒攻击（poisoning attacks），即对抗性文档被注入以操纵生成器的输出。现有检测方法依赖输出端信号（如困惑度和一致性检查），但分析发现蓄意攻击往往诱发虚假置信度（false confidence），被投毒的输出甚至比正常输出具有更低的困惑度，这使得基于不确定性的检测方法失效。

Method: 作者首先分析生成器内部动态，识别出一种名为"Attention Collapse"（注意力坍缩）的特征信号——正常生成中注意力是分散的，而受攻击的生成中注意力会集中到被投毒的文档上，导致熵降低。基于此发现，提出D-SCAN（Document-level Signal Collapse Analysis），一个轻量级检测框架，通过监控注意力动态来识别受攻击的生成。

Result: 在多个攻击基准上的广泛实验验证了D-SCAN的有效性。此外，D-SCAN即使在攻击未能改变最终答案的情况下也能检测出攻击，展示了其鲁棒性和灵敏性。

Conclusion: 通过从输出端信号转向内部注意力动态，D-SCAN为RAG投毒攻击检测提供了一种新颖且有效的思路，能够捕捉传统方法无法察觉的微妙攻击痕迹。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is indispensable for enhancing large language models. However, RAGs are increasingly susceptible to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents are injected to manipulate generator outputs. Previous methods rely on output-side signals such as perplexity and consistency checks to detect such attacks. Nevertheless, our analysis reveals that deliberate attacks often induce false confidence, where poisoned outputs exhibit even lower perplexity than benign ones, rendering uncertainty-based detection ineffective. To address this challenge, we explore the internal dynamics of the generator and identify a distinctive signature termed \textit{Attention Collapse}. Unlike the dispersed attention in benign generations, attacked generations exhibit a decrease in entropy as attention concentrates on poisoned documents. Building on these findings, we propose \texttt{D-SCAN} (Document-level Signal Collapse Analysis), a lightweight detection framework that monitors attention dynamics to identify attacked generations. Extensive experiments on multiple attack benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. Moreover, D-SCAN can detect attacks even when they fail to alter the final answer. Code is available at https://github.com/yingtaoren/D-Scan.git.

</details>


### [161] [Casting the Net! Revisiting MasterFace Impersonation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06952)
*Seunghun Paik,Sunpill Kim,Chanwoo Hwang,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于商业人脸识别API的MasterFaces攻击方法，通过将攻击形式化为生物特征表示空间上的最大覆盖问题（称为NET），攻击者仅凭合法的公开API访问权限即可在30次认证尝试内将冒名顶替率提升至标准FMR的9.5倍，突破此前被认为MasterFaces在现代FRS中无效的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统攻击向量在现实对抗能力限制下（如有限的决策级认证尝试次数、无内部系统知识）大多不可行，零努力冒名顶替（FMR）被视为唯一基线。虽然MasterFaces攻击曾被提出，但后续研究认为其在现代FRS中无法超越标准FMR。然而，现实世界中许多FRS部署基于商业API构建，且后端服务商可被公开获知或推断，这一观察为攻击者提供了新的可行途径，因此作者重新审视MasterFaces攻击的可行性。

Method: 作者将MasterFaces攻击形式化为生物特征表示空间上的最大覆盖问题（Maximum Coverage Problem），称为NET（Neighborhood-Encompassing Templates）。攻击者通过购买商业API服务（无需额外权限），利用表示空间的几何结构构造API定制的NET，选择一组MasterFaces以最大化覆盖目标FRS的认证阈值区域。该方法针对基于商业API的下游应用进行定向攻击。

Result: 实验表明，该攻击在多个开源和商业API-based人脸识别系统上将冒名顶替率提升至标准FMR预期的9.5倍，且最多仅需30次认证尝试即可实现。

Conclusion: 商业API的公开可用性使得MasterFaces攻击在现代FRS中重新成为现实威胁，突破了FMR基线。这揭示了基于商业API构建的人脸识别下游应用存在未被充分认识的安全隐患，需要重新评估其威胁模型和防护策略。

Abstract: Impersonation is a fundamental security threat in face recognition systems (FRSs). While the security of FRSs has been challenged by various attack vectors, under realistic adversarial capabilities, e.g., a limited number of decision-only authentication trials and no internal system knowledge, most attack techniques become infeasible. As a result, impersonation by zero-effort impostors, characterized by false match rate (FMR), is commonly regarded as a standalone baseline. A few years ago, impersonation attacks based on MasterFaces emerged as a notable security threat that could break the barrier of the FMR-based baseline under such realistic constraints. However, they were believed not to yield impersonation above the standard FMR in modern FRSs, as discussed by multiple follow-up studies. In this paper, we demonstrate that even legitimate access to public commercial APIs allows an adversary to amplify impersonation rates through MasterFaces, resulting in a non-trivial impersonation attack beyond FMR on downstream applications built on top of these APIs. We observe that several real-world FRS deployments are implemented using commercial APIs, and that the backend service provider is publicly disclosed or trivially inferable. As a result, the adversary can purchase these pay-as-you-go API services without requiring any additional privilege over the target FRS. From this observation, we formalize the MasterFaces attack as a maximum coverage problem over the biometric representation space, which we call a NET, and show that the adversary can construct an API-tailored NET by leveraging the geometric structure of the representation space. We demonstrate that our attack amplifies the impersonation rates of several open-source and commercial API-based FRSs by up to 9.5$\times$ within at most 30 authentication trials, compared to those expected from the standard FMR.

</details>


### [162] [HarnessSafe: Evaluating Safety Across Persistent Carriers in Agent Harnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.06984)
*Xiao Zhang,Yusheng Wang,Yuhao Fei,Dongyuan Li,Zian Liang,Liuyu Xiang,Hongxun Gu,Zhaofeng He*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HarnessSafe基准测试，包含328个可执行案例，覆盖七种持久化载体家族，用于系统性评估智能体框架中通过持久化载体传播的延迟安全风险。该基准引入了持久化风险生命周期（Persistent-Risk Lifecycle）规范和基于痕迹的多阶段评估方法，实验发现风险遏制效果具有载体特定性，且强烈依赖于框架和模型配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能体通过记忆、技能、工具和共享工件等持久化载体跨任务和会话保持状态，这引入了延迟安全风险：攻击者影响的内容可能跨越系统边界，在后续良性请求执行时产生危害。现有基准通常只关注少数载体或框架，且端到端攻击成功率无法揭示风险如何在系统中传播和演化。

Method: 作者构建了HarnessSafe基准，包含328个可执行案例，覆盖七种持久化载体家族，并在主流智能体框架上进行评估。每个案例被规范化为持久化风险生命周期，追踪攻击者影响从初始注入、经载体和系统边界持久化、到后续良性触发及可观察违规的完整链路。同时提出了多阶段、基于痕迹的评估方法，使用可观察的执行证据判断每条攻击链的进展程度及被阻断的位置。

Result: 实验表明风险遏制具有载体特定性，且强烈依赖于框架-模型的配置组合。框架和后端模型均显著影响遏制结果，而攻击成功率无法反映不同生命周期阶段的进展模式差异。

Conclusion: 攻击成功率的单一指标不足以评估智能体框架的持久化安全风险，需要基于风险生命周期的多阶段追踪评估来揭示风险传播和遏制的具体机制。风险遏制需要在框架和模型层面协同设计与配置。

Abstract: Modern agent harnesses persist state across tasks and sessions through persistent carriers like memory, skills, tools, and shared artifacts. However, this capability creates delayed safety risks: attacker-influenced content can cross system boundaries and later affect the execution of a benign request. Existing benchmarks typically focus on a few carriers or harnesses, while end-to-end attack-success rates reveal little about how risks propagate. To this end, we present HarnessSafe, a benchmark comprising 328 executable cases across seven persistent-carrier families and evaluated on most mainstream agent harnesses. Each case is specified as a Persistent-Risk Lifecycle that traces attacker influence from its initial entry, through persistence across carriers and system boundaries, to a later benign trigger and an observable violation. We further introduce a multi-stage, trace-based evaluation that uses observable execution evidence to determine how far each attack chain progresses and where it is stopped. Experiments show that containment is carrier-specific and strongly depends on the harness-model configuration. Both the harness and model backend substantially shape containment outcomes, while attack success rates cannot reflect distinct lifecycle progression patterns.

</details>


### [163] [Effects of parental controls in the context of Digital Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07016)
*Selina Märchya,Mauro Vignatia,Frank Breitinger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究实证评估了微软、谷歌和苹果三大厂商的家长控制系统对数字取证过程的影响，通过对15台Windows、Android和iOS设备进行受控实验，分析了这些系统对证据可获取性的阻碍，并提出了具有取证学可靠性的规避方法，为调查人员提供实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家长控制系统本质上是为保护未成年人上网安全而设计，但启用后会限制管理员权限、禁用调试选项并改变数据可访问性，这些限制严重妨碍了数字取证调查中的证据获取与分析工作。然而，现有研究缺乏对主流家长控制系统取证影响的系统性实证评估，导致调查人员面临取证困难时缺乏指导。

Method: 本研究选取微软、谷歌和苹果三大厂商的家长控制系统，在十五台分别运行Windows、Android和iOS系统的设备上进行受控实验。通过对比家长控制启用前后的取证流程，系统评估这些系统对证据可获取性的具体影响，并实验性地测试和验证在保持取证完整性（forensic soundness）前提下克服这些限制的可行方法。

Result: 研究揭示了三大主流家长控制系统在取证层面造成的主要阻碍（包括权限限制、调试功能禁用、数据访问路径改变等），同时验证了若干在取证学上可靠的突破方法，使调查人员能够在家长控制启用的环境中成功完成证据获取与分析。

Conclusion: 家长控制系统对数字取证构成实质性障碍，但通过恰当的取证方法可以加以克服。本文为在家长控制管制的设备环境中开展取证调查提供了实证依据和实用操作指南，同时有助于提升此类场景下的取证就绪能力（forensic readiness）。

Abstract: Parental control systems are designed to protect minors online, but can inadvertently obstruct digital forensic investigations. When enabled, these systems restrict administrative privileges, disable debugging options, and alter data accessibility, complicating evidence acquisition and analysis. This study empirically examines the impact of Microsoft, Google, and Apple parental controls on forensic processes across fifteen Windows, Android, and iOS devices. Through controlled experiments, we evaluate their impact on evidence accessibility and identify forensically sound methods to overcome these limitations. The findings provide practical guidance for investigators and contribute to improving forensic readiness in environments governed by parental control systems.

</details>


### [164] [Soft Redaction of Image Provenance via Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07063)
*Muhammad Awan,John Collomosse*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了图像来源（Provenance）的"软编辑"机制：用零知识证明（ZKP）替换敏感的来源断言，在不泄露底层数据的前提下证明所选属性（如距离、相似性），兼容C2PA标准，构建仅需数秒、验证仅需毫秒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 内容来源标准（如C2PA）通过签名记录增强图像可信度，但这些来源透明性与隐私之间存在冲突——增强信任的断言可能泄露创作人的敏感信息或拍摄背景。需要一种在不破坏来源可信度的前提下保护隐私的机制。

Method: 提出"软编辑"机制：用ZKP替换敏感来源断言，支持三类距离证明：（1）位置断言支持到公共参考点的接近性证明，基于切比雪夫多项式近似实现于ZKP电路中；（2）扩展到生物特征嵌入上的L2距离证明，实现人像相似性隐私保护声明，用于人格权执行；（3）将同一距离证明构造应用于感知哈希，支持基于水印恢复被剥离来源元数据的反欺骗用例。整体兼容C2PA。

Result: 实验证明该方法可提供实际的软编辑能力，兼容C2PA，ZKP构造仅需数秒，验证仅需毫秒级时间。

Conclusion: ZKP能够为图像来源提供实用的软编辑能力，在保持来源可信度的同时保护隐私，且性能开销可接受。

Abstract: Content provenance standards, such as C2PA, are increasingly used to attach signed records of origin, editing history, and rights to digital images. However, provenance transparency can conflict with privacy -- assertions that strengthen trust in an image may also reveal sensitive information about the creator or capture context. We propose soft redaction for image provenance: a mechanism that replaces sensitive provenance assertions with zero-knowledge proofs (ZKPs) of selected properties over hidden data. Our work focuses on distance proofs. We first show how location assertions can support proofs of proximity to a public reference point, using Chebyshev polynomial approximations within the ZKP proof circuit. We then extend the approach to L2 distance proofs over biometric embeddings, enabling privacy-preserving claims related to likeness to help enforce personality rights with images. Finally, we apply the same distance-proof construction to perceptual hashes (visual fingerprints), supporting an anti-spoofing use case in watermark-based recovery of stripped provenance metadata. Our results demonstrate that ZKPs over image provenance can provide practical soft-redaction capabilities, compatible with C2PA, that may be constructed in seconds and verified in milliseconds.

</details>


### [165] [SoK: Cryptographic Key Recovery for Cryptoasset Custody and Financial Technologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07104)
*Francisco Javier Becerra Sanchez,Antonio Ken Iannillo,Radu State*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是关于加密资产托管与金融技术中加密密钥恢复的系统化知识综述（SoK），基于118篇发现语料提炼出77篇合成语料，构建了涵盖多维度属性的主矩阵，提出了轴优先分类法划分出五类恢复方案，并提出了广义构造模型及六项核心发现，最终明确了恢复感知型金融技术的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加密资产系统中密钥控制与财务控制紧密绑定，丢失钱包种子、托管份额、硬件设备或智能账户凭证会导致花费权限丧失，而受损的恢复机制可能导致资产被盗。现有研究使用彼此独立的不同术语框架（如密钥备份、秘密共享、账户恢复、凭证重发、社交恢复、资产迁移）来讨论恢复问题，使得机制间的比较和权衡变得困难，亟需一个统一的系统化框架来整合与分析该领域。

Method: 从118篇系统综述发现语料出发，筛选出77篇综合语料；在包含已恢复对象、恢复语义、机制、注册与存储、授权、信任放置、故障事件、恢复后状态、验证证据、部署状态、隐私、可用性与局限性等多维度的主矩阵中对每篇论文进行编码；基于该矩阵提出轴优先分类法，将恢复方案划分为秘密恢复型、混合型、控制恢复型、取证/提取型与框架导向型五类；进一步推导广义构造模型并与生产级设计进行对照验证。

Result: 恢复并非单一操作，系统可能重建原始秘密、重新生成种子、恢复共享、重发凭证、迁移签名权限、恢复账户控制、转移资产或提取取证工件。通过广义构造模型与生产级设计的对照，得出六项发现：恢复语义具有异构性；恢复会转移信任；活性改进创造滥用路径；恢复后生命周期管理不均衡；协议证据优于用户证据；恢复元数据保护不足。

Conclusion: 上述六项发现揭示了当前密钥恢复研究的系统性缺口，并为恢复感知型金融技术提出了研究议程，推动该领域从碎片化向统一理论和方法论发展。

Abstract: Cryptoasset systems often bind cryptographic key control to financial control: losing a wallet seed, custody share, hardware device, or smart-account credential can remove spend authority, while compromised recovery can enable theft. Existing work treats recovery through separate vocabularies--key backup, secret sharing, account recovery, credential re-issuance, social recovery, and asset migration--making mechanisms and tradeoffs difficult to compare.
  This paper presents a Systematization of Knowledge (SoK) on cryptographic key recovery for cryptoasset custody and financial technologies. Starting from a 118-paper systematic-review discovery corpus, we derive a 77-paper synthesis corpus and code each retained system in a master matrix covering recovered objects, recovery semantics, mechanisms, enrollment and storage, authorization, trust placement, failure events, post-recovery state, validation evidence, deployment status, privacy, usability, and limitations. The matrix supports an axis-first taxonomy that separates secret-restoring, hybrid, control-restoring, forensic/extractive, and framework-oriented recovery.
  Our central observation is that recovery is not a single operation: systems may reconstruct an original secret, regenerate a seed, restore a share, reissue a credential, migrate signing authority, restore account control, move assets, or extract forensic artifacts. We derive a generalized construction model, check it against production-facing designs, and identify six findings: recovery semantics are heterogeneous; recovery shifts trust; liveness improvements create abuse paths; post-recovery lifecycle management is uneven; protocol evidence outpaces user evidence; and recovery metadata remains underprotected. These gaps motivate a research agenda for recovery-aware financial technologies.

</details>


### [166] ["Operator, can you hear me?" A Faithful Line into the UNISOC Baseband](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07143)
*Eduard Vlad,Philipp Mao,Marcel Busch,Mathias Payer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Unislop方法，通过从真实设备建模基带处理器及其周围组件（协处理器、SIM、应用处理器），并以共享时钟与基带锁步运行，实现对UNISOC UDX710基带固件的系统化重托管分析，成功到达控制面状态、建立完整PDU会话并承载真实IP流量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基带处理器通过无线电始终可达，其控制面处理器中负责注册、认证和会话建立的安全关键逻辑位于协议状态机深处。系统化分析这些逻辑需要对运行中的固件进行内省，因此必须对基带进行重托管。然而现有重托管方法对执行环境过度近似、对基带处理器及其周围组件的SoC复杂度欠近似，导致系统化分析实际上不可行。

Method: Unislop方法从真实设备行为出发，为每个周围组件（协处理器、SIM、应用处理器）建模，并让这些组件与基带处理器在单一共享时钟上锁步运行。这使得保真度可以在组件接口层面被检查而非假设。具体流程包括：从Quectel RM500U-CNV模块获得代码执行权限、绕过固件完整性检查、插桩基带、从运行设备恢复外围环境。

Result: 生成的re-host达到了与真实设备相同的控制面状态，建立了完整的PDU会话，并在入向和出向上承载了真实IP流量。研究还发现UDX710平台约占蜂窝调制解调器的10-15%且用于汽车系统，此前未被系统分析过。恢复的组件在UNISOC基带产品线中共享，通过额外逆向工程可将该设计扩展到更多目标。

Conclusion: Unislop通过组件级建模和锁步执行克服了基带重托管的保真度难题，首次对UNISOC UDX710平台进行了系统性安全分析，证明了实际可到达基带控制面状态并承载真实流量的能力，为大规模基带固件分析铺平了道路。

Abstract: Baseband processors are reachable over the radio at all times. Their most security-relevant logic runs deep inside protocol state machines: the control-plane handlers that gate registration, authentication, and session setup. Analyzing that logic systematically requires introspecting the firmware as it runs, which makes re-hosting the baseband necessary. Existing re-hosting work approximates the execution environment and under-approximates the SoC complexity of the baseband processor together with its surrounding components, bringing this state practically out of reach. We instead model each surrounding component, co-processors, SIM, application processor, from what a real device does, and step them in lockstep with the baseband on one shared clock. That makes faithfulness checkable at component interfaces, rather than assumed.
  We call this method Unislop and demonstrate it on the UNISOC UDX710, a platform in an estimated 10-15% of cellular modems and in automotive systems, not systematically analyzed before. Starting from a Quectel RM500U-CNV module, we gain code execution, defeat its firmware-integrity check, instrument the baseband, and recover its peripheral environment from the running device. The resulting re-host reaches the same control-plane states as the real device, establishes a full PDU session, and carries real IP traffic on both ingress and egress. The recovered components are shared across UNISOC's baseband lineup, so with additional reverse-engineering effort the same design extends to further targets.

</details>


### [167] [An Analysis of Architectural and Operational Dynamics of Phishkits in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.07451)
*Behzad Ousat,Mohammad Ali Tofighi,Estefan Schafir,Amin Kharraz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对2020至2023年间收集的1,300个现代网络钓鱼工具包（phishkits）进行了全面分析，研究其架构、源代码、通信渠道及泄露数据类型。研究发现高级功能仅占少数，21.8%的工具包不采用任何规避机制；大量代码复用和已知技巧使得钓鱼工具包具有高度可预测性，有利于大规模检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钓鱼攻击长期是攻击者欺骗用户、绕过现代防御机制并渗透关键系统的重要手段。phishkits作为构建和部署成功钓鱼攻击的关键要素，能够帮助生成诱人的钓鱼页面、实施规避策略并建立与远程攻击者的后门通信渠道。然而，对此类现代工具包的系统性分析尚不充分，需要深入理解其架构与运作机制以增强防御能力。

Method: 收集并分析了2020至2023年间获取的1,300个现代phishkits。分析维度包括：工具包架构、源代码、通信通道、与对手共享的泄露数据性质；识别了动态重定向和入站网络流量归因等规避与隐藏机制；以及使用当前消息服务交换被盗数据的模式。

Result: 分析发现：部分phishkits具备动态重定向和流量归因等规避/隐藏机制；大量工具包依赖现代消息服务交换被盗数据；但具备高级功能的phishkits数量很少，其中284个（21.8%）完全未使用任何规避机制。虽然各工具包在实现细节上存在差异，但维持钓鱼页面功能的核心组件高度相似甚至完全相同。

Conclusion: phishkits存在高度的代码复用和对已知技巧的依赖，导致大量案例具有可预测性，这为在更大规模上检测这些对抗性操作提供了便利条件。

Abstract: Phishing attacks have always been a favored vector for adversaries to defraud users, bypass modern defense mechanisms, and penetrate critical systems. Among all the elements contributing to the creation and deployment of successful phishing attacks, phishkits stand out as a crucial parameter. Phishkits often facilitate creating and deploying compelling phishing pages, implement evasion strategies, and establish and maintain backdoors with remote adversaries for exchanging leaked data. In this work, we performed an analysis of 1,300 modern phishkits collected from 2020 to 2023. We analyzed the architecture, source code, communication channels, and the nature of leaked data shared with adversaries. We identified mechanisms for dynamic redirection and attributing incoming web traffic as part of the evasion and cloaking mechanism. We also observed heavy reliance on current messaging services for exchanging stolen data with phishers. That said, our analysis shows that the number of phishkits with advanced functionalities is quite small. We identified 284 (21.8%) phishkits that did not use any form of evasion mechanism. We also observed that while there were differences in the implementation details of phishkits, the major components that keep phishing pages functional were very similar or even identical across kits. The level of code reuse and heavy reliance on known tricks to build pre-packaged phishing pages make a large number of cases predictable, which can potentially make the detection of these adversarial operations even easier at scale.

</details>
